\maketitle

\begin{abstract}Every move below was produced by relational analogy to named master games (human and/or top-engine -- the exact source is named per move). Each figure shows our position with Melanie's move (a) and the top retrieved analog positions (b--d) with the master move played there; the caption names the exact source and the piece-role correspondence under $\mu$ -- cross-type maps (a rook playing a queen's role) are called out, as no material/eval engine explains a move that way.\end{abstract}

\caption{\textbf{Move 7, White: \texttt{b3}.} (a) Our position; Melanie plays \texttt{b3} (\texttt{b2b3}). Panels (b)--(d) are the retrieved master positions whose correspondence most drove this move (highest QAP objective among those that transfer to it). (b) Retrieved from \texttt{lichess\_elite\_2024-09\_p0003\#2242} (ply 23) [structural distance $d$=73, QAP obj=25]: the master played \texttt{Nbd7}; under $\mu$ our \emph{Pb2} plays the role of that game's \emph{Nb1} (a \textbf{cross-type} correspondence); cross-type roles: our \emph{q}d8 as their \emph{P}e7, our \emph{n}b8 as their \emph{P}h7; transferred move \texttt{b2b3} \textbf{(endorses this move)}. (c) Retrieved from \texttt{lichess\_elite\_2024-09\_p0024\#953} (ply 24) [structural distance $d$=63, QAP obj=58]: the master played \texttt{b5}; under $\mu$ our \emph{Pb2} plays the role of that game's \emph{Pb4}; cross-type roles: our \emph{Q}d1 as their \emph{R}b1, our \emph{B}g2 as their \emph{Q}d1; transferred move \texttt{b2b4}. (d) Retrieved from \texttt{lichess\_elite\_2024-09\_p0013\#3379} (ply 20) [structural distance $d$=71, QAP obj=52]: the master played \texttt{Ne5}; under $\mu$ our \emph{Bc1} plays the role of that game's \emph{Nf3} (a \textbf{cross-type} correspondence); cross-type roles: our \emph{q}d8 as their \emph{R}a1, our \emph{b}d6 as their \emph{N}b1; transferred move \texttt{c1e3}. Synthesis: 1 retrieved analog transferred to \texttt{b3} (summed QAP objective 25, the strongest correspondence among the candidate moves); Melanie committed it via the \texttt{search} selector.}
\label{fig:thought-m12}
\end{figure}
